## [Vintage Market Days of Colorado Springs](/events-1/vintage-market-days-of-colorado-springs-12)

*    11/22/2025 10:00 AM - 11/22/2025 04:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Vintage Market Days® is an upscale vintage-inspired indoor/outdoor market featuring original art, antiques, clothing, jewelry, handmade treasures, home décor, outdoor furnishings, consumable yummies, seasonal plantings and a little more. Vintage Market Days® events are so much more than a flea market. Each Vintage Market Days® event is a unique opportunity for vendors to display their talents and passions in creative venues. Ticket details coming soon.

## [Light Up the Night for Colorado Gives Day](/events-1/light-up-the-night-for-colorado-gives-day)

*    12/2/2025 06:00 PM - 12/2/2025 06:30 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Join Colorado Gives Foundation as we "Light Up the Night" with a free, family-friendly drive-in laser show experience. Let's celebrate the amazing nonprofits participating in Colorado Gives Day! Park your car and enjoy a dazzling 20-minute experience with complimentary popcorn, featuring a massive 30-foot screen, immersive lasers and music perfectly synchronized to stunning visuals. It’s a vibrant evening designed to inspire excitement as Colorado comes together for its biggest day of giving. Click the link to reserve your spot now.

[Buy Now](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/light-up-the-night-for-colorado-gives-day-drive-in-laser-show-cosprngs-tickets-1839280691059?aff=erelpanelorg)[Read More](/events-1/light-up-the-night-for-colorado-gives-day)

*     1125 West Moreno Avenue, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

## [27th Annual Holiday Food & Gift Festival](/events-1/27th-annual-holiday-food-gift-festival)

*   **$8.00**
*    12/5/2025 10:00 AM - 12/5/2025 05:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

The 27th Annual Holiday Food & Gift Festival returns to Colorado Springs December 5-7 at the Norris Penrose Event Center. This Festival is one of the largest holiday celebrations in the West, with shoppers who happily complete their holiday shopping early. With over 150 exhibitors, the festival will provide shoppers with one-of-a-kind gifts. Our show proudly supports small businesses, featuring arts, crafts, woodworking, wearable items, Christmas décor and the show hosts an extensive gourmet food selection and much more. 🎟️ Admission is good for all three days. 🧑‍🎄 Santa will be at the show both Saturday and Sunday. 🎶 Live music will be happening on Saturday. Enjoy a 40’ train – scavenger hunt open to all – handled by the train association Enjoy the Hot Cocoa bar! 🍫 This is the event to Sip, Shop & Sample

[Buy Now](https://hfgf.com/project/colorado-springs-holiday-festival-show/)[Read More](/events-1/27th-annual-holiday-food-gift-festival)

## [Pikes Peak Sports Card Show](/events-1/pikes-peak-sports-card-show-17)

*    12/6/2025 09:00 AM - 12/6/2025 04:00 PM
*     945 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Southern Colorado's longest running card and collectibles show. Buy-Sell-Trade baseball, basketball, football, hockey, soccer, racing, Pokemon, Magic, One Piece, TCG, Star Wars, Funko Pops, collectibles, memorabilia and so much more. 120 vendor tables, open to the public, family friendly environment, FREE admission. Located in the Indoor Extension at Norris Penrose Event Center.

## [2nd Annual Candy Cane Craft & Gift Show](/events-1/2nd-annual-candy-cane-craft-gift-show)

*    12/13/2025 10:00 AM - 12/13/2025 05:00 PM
*     We have moved the event to the amazing Norris Penrose Event Center, located between 8th and 21st s1045 Lower Gold Camp Rd, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

We invite you to the 2nd annual Candy Cane Craft & Gift Show, hosted by CSLE, supporting the city’s small non-profit sector. The event will be held at the Norris Penrose Event Center on Saturday, December 13 and Sunday, December 14. Attendance is FREE to the public and attendees can browse 140 Colorado vendors offering handmade jewelry, textiles, home décor, as well as a range of gift options. The show celebrates local art, community, and philanthropy through crafts by area artists. Activities are available for all age groups including: DIY UGLY Sweater Contests; Gingerbread House Building Contests; opportunities to meet Santa and Mrs. Claus; Live Reindeer interactions; a Hot Cocoa Bar; Mrs. Claus' Bakery; Dance Performances; Music; Face Painting and access to a Photo Booth. Proceeds will go to Harley’s Hope Foundation, which provides services aimed at maintaining pet-family relationships, with programs focused on financial assistance for veterinary care and additional support for senior pet owners. For more information, click the 'Read More' button.

[Buy Now](https://www.candycaneshowcase.com/)[Read More](/events-1/2nd-annual-candy-cane-craft-gift-show)

01Jan

](/events-1/el-paso-county-search-rescue-s-48th-annual-rescue-run)

## [El Paso County Search & Rescue's 48th Annual Rescue Run](/events-1/el-paso-county-search-rescue-s-48th-annual-rescue-run)

*    1/1/2026 10:00 AM - 1/1/2026 12:00 PM
*     945 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Start the New Year off with the 48th Annual Rescue Run! Join us on the wide trails in East Bear Creek Park. We have a 5K and a 10K. Both races start at 10:00am. Plan to cross the start line no later than 10:10am. Please sign up early to assure shirt availability. Supply delays may limit the opportunity for an additional order. Expect snow and ice to be on the trails. Wear traction at your own discretion. Proceeds of this event go to El Paso County Search & Rescue (EPCSAR). EPCSAR provides search and rescue services primarily in the jurisdiction of El Paso County, Colorado. However, the team also responds to requests for assistance from other counties through a cooperative arrangement with the Colorado Search and Rescue Board. Online registration ends on December 31st, 2025 at 6:00pm.

[Buy Now](https://runsignup.com/Race/CO/ColoradoSprings/RescueRun5K10K)[Read More](/events-1/el-paso-county-search-rescue-s-48th-annual-rescue-run)

## [Home & Garden Show](/events-1/home-garden-show)

*    2/6/2026 12:00 PM - 2/6/2026 06:00 PM
*     945 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Welcome to the Home and Garden Show. This comprehensive event brings together homeowners and many of the most knowledgeable and experienced remodeling, landscaping and building experts. Every aspect of the home can be explored, from the smallest design detail to the largest house addition. Professionals will be on-hand to share their valuable knowledge to bring your ideas to life or inspire you with new ones. See the latest innovations and design trends! Experts will be showcasing everything for the home including, but not limited to, the latest in cabinetry and countertops, flooring, sunrooms and additions, basement finishing, waterproofing, smart home automation, energy efficient windows and exterior products, as well as the newest ideas pertaining to landscape installations. Be inspired by the feature gardens built right inside the exhibit hall - a great precursor to spring! Make plans to visit with the most knowledgeable experts in the area at the Home and Garden Show. This all-encompassing event will surely put you on the path towards making your dream home a reality. As an added bonus, parking is free. We look forward to seeing you at the Norris Penrose Event Center this February!

[Buy Now](https://www.homeshowcenter.com/overview/coloradospringhome2)[Read More](/events-1/home-garden-show)

## [Tanner Gun Show](/events-1/tanner-gun-show-39)

*   **$15.00**
*    10/12/2025 10:00 AM - 10/12/2025 04:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

The Tanner Gun Show has been around since 1964 and has expanded into Colorado Springs. At the Colorado Springs show, you’ll find hundreds of vendor tables with all types of guns, knives, ammunition, survival gear and food, and related hunting and fishing accessories including: Holsters Western Wear Gun Safes Books War Relics Other Collectibles We attract vendors from throughout the Western half of the U.S., so you are guaranteed the largest selection of used and new firearms in the state. From the coolest gadgets and technologies to antiques and collector’s items, you’ll find it all here. Although the focus of the show is guns, knives, and outdoor equipment, there are also some other items for those not interested in guns, such as beef jerky, western wear, jewelry, artwork, and books. There is a restaurant on-site with a large seating area. DON’T FORGET THAT ALL SHOWS ARE BUY, SELL, & TRADE! CUSTOMERS ARE ENCOURAGED TO BRING IN THEIR GUNS TO SELL, TRADE, OR GET AN APPRAISAL. PLENTY OF FREE PARKING AVAILABLE! Strollers must be checked at the door.

[Read More](/events-1/tanner-gun-show-39)

## [Guns & Hoses City Slicker Rodeo](/events-1/guns-hoses-city-slicker-rodeo)

*   **$20.00**
*    6/28/2025 06:00 PM - 6/28/2025 09:00 PM
*     1045 LOWER GOLD CAMP RD

Join us for the first-ever annual Guns & Hoses City Slicker Rodeo in Colorado Springs at the Norris Penrose Event Center. The rodeo's contestants will be composed of local Law Enforcement, First Responders, and Military Teams. All proceeds go to local charities that support Law Enforcement, First Responders, and the Military. This is a 501(c) nonprofit event. The El Paso County Sheriff’s Office is teaming up with the Norris Penrose Rodeo Legacy Foundation and C&L Productions to celebrate our western heritage and honor our first responders and military members in the Pikes Peak Region by hosting our first annual Guns and Hoses City Slickers Ranch Rodeo. No Cowboy skills will be needed from the team members, just some good ol’ fashion grit and determination to represent their profession! Tickets go on sale March 14th, 2025 at 12:01AM.

## [Tanner Gun Show](/events-1/tanner-gun-show-36)

*   **$15**
*    6/22/2025 10:00 AM - 6/22/2025 04:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

The Tanner Gun Show has been around since 1964. At the Colorado Springs show, you’ll find hundreds of vendor tables with all types of guns, knives, ammunition, survival gear and food, and related hunting and fishing accessories. We attract vendors from throughout the Western half of the U.S., so you are guaranteed the largest selection of used and new firearms in the state. From the coolest gadgets and technologies to antiques and collector’s items, you’ll find it all here. The focus of the show is guns, knives, and outdoor equipment, there are also other items for those not interested in guns, such as beef jerky, western wear, jewelry, artwork, and books. ALL SHOWS ARE BUY, SELL, & TRADE!

## [Pikes Peak Gem, Mineral, and Jewelry Show](/events-1/pikes-peak-gem-mineral-and-jewelry-show-2)

*   **$5.00**
*    6/8/2025 10:00 AM - 6/8/2025 04:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Explore a dazzling array of gems, minerals, fossils, and unique hand-crafted jewelry pieces. Discover stunning pieces from talented artisans and vendors, learn about the fascinating worlds of minerals, fossils, jewelry making, and rockhounding, and find the perfect piece to add to your collection! The 2025 show will host over 55 vendors specializing in rocks and minerals from Colorado and other US and international localities, as well as fossils, meteorites, sculptures, jewelry, crystals, and more! Young prospectors will enjoy gold panning, exploring and learning through a scavenger hunt, and connecting with our Pebble Pups group. Beading and wire wrapping classes will be offered throughout the weekend. To sign up for classes click 'Read More'.

07Jun

](/events-1/pikes-peak-gem-mineral-and-jewelry-show-1)

## [Pikes Peak Gem, Mineral, and Jewelry Show](/events-1/pikes-peak-gem-mineral-and-jewelry-show-1)

*   **$5.00**
*    6/7/2025 10:00 AM - 6/7/2025 06:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Explore a dazzling array of gems, minerals, fossils, and unique hand-crafted jewelry pieces. Discover stunning pieces from talented artisans and vendors, learn about the fascinating worlds of minerals, fossils, jewelry making, and rockhounding, and find the perfect piece to add to your collection! The 2025 show will host over 55 vendors specializing in rocks and minerals from Colorado and other US and international localities, as well as fossils, meteorites, sculptures, jewelry, crystals, and more! Young prospectors will enjoy gold panning, exploring and learning through a scavenger hunt, and connecting with our Pebble Pups group. Beading and wire wrapping classes will be offered throughout the weekend. To sign up for classes click 'Read More'.

[Read More](/events-1/pikes-peak-gem-mineral-and-jewelry-show-1)

## [Vintage Market Days](/events-1/vintage-market-days-7)

*    6/1/2025 10:00 AM - 6/1/2025 04:00 PM
*     1045 LOWER GOLD CAMP RD

Vintage Market Days® is an upscale vintage-inspired indoor/outdoor market featuring original art, antiques, clothing, jewelry, handmade treasures, home décor, outdoor furnishings, consumable yummies, seasonal plantings and a little more. Vintage Market Days® events are so much more than a flea market. Each Vintage Market Days® event is a unique opportunity for vendors to display their talents and passions in creative venues. Tickets are available at the gate. Click 'Read More' to find out how to become a vendor.

17May

](/events-1/2nd-annual-touch-a-truck)

## [2nd Annual Touch-A-Truck](/events-1/2nd-annual-touch-a-truck)

*    5/17/2025 09:00 AM - 5/17/2025 01:00 PM
*     1045 LOWER GOLD CAMP RD

Join us for Touch-A-Truck, presented by Woven Care Pediatric Therapy, at the Norris-Penrose Indoor Event Center; where kids of all ages can sit in, play on, and flash the lights of their favorite vehicles! Garbage Trucks, construction vehicles, first responders, military and community vehicles from all corners of Colorado Springs will be present to let your kiddos explore. We will even have helicopters (weather permitting)! This event runs from 9 AM - 1 PM, and is absolutely free! No tickets required; stop by anytime during the event to experience the fun. New this year: Touch-A-Truck will be completely horn-free (parents please help us in this endeavor) to accommodate those with noise sensitivities. We have partnered with the Children's Entrepreneur Market where kids from all over our community will be on hand to sell their amazing homemade items! Click 'Read More' if you would like to help sponsor this event.

[Read More](/events-1/2nd-annual-touch-a-truck)

10May

](/events-1/march-for-heroes)

## [March for Heroes](/events-1/march-for-heroes)

*   **$40.00**
*    5/10/2025 05:30 AM - 5/10/2025 05:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

This RUCK is a 30K march (walk/run) with a rucksack weighing 11 kg at both the start and finish line. Dry non-perishable goods can be used for the 24.25 lbs. and donated at the end of the event to fight hunger in our community. Active-duty, veterans, and civilians are all welcome to participate in this challenging event. Finish within the allotted time to be awarded the coveted Norwegian Foot March Badge and Certificate!

[Read More](/events-1/march-for-heroes)

## [Hot Tub & Swim Spa Sale](/events-1/hot-tub-swim-spa-sale-45)

*    5/4/2025 10:00 AM - 5/4/2025 06:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Stop by the indoor event center to browse the latest hot tubs with a vast selection in sizes, shapes, features and colors to fit any lifestyle, backyard, or budget. Choose from items in stock or build-to-order a custom spa. See firsthand the propulsion, depth and feature differences of over a dozen models of Swim Spas from 11 feet to over 19 feet! Hot tubs are excellent for your health and wellbeing. They offer a combination of hydrotherapy jets, massage, and warm water which can be beneficial in increasing blood flow and help you feel better whether you want to unwind, enjoy better sleep, or soothe aching and sore muscles.

[Read More](/events-1/hot-tub-swim-spa-sale-45)

12Apr

](/events-1/tillman-honor-run)

## [Tillman Honor Run](/events-1/tillman-honor-run)

*    4/12/2025 10:00 AM - 4/12/2025 01:00 PM
*     1045 LOWER GOLD CAMP RD

The ASU Alumni Association, in support of the Pat Tillman Foundation, is proud to coordinate Tillman Honor Runs, hosted by ASU Alumni Chapters & Clubs around the United States. On April 12 (April 5, April 19, and April 26 for select cities), join us to run or walk 4.2 miles and raise money to support the Tillman Scholar program. Tillman Honor Runs are casual, family-friendly, in-person fun runs. Participate and register in one of the following cities. We welcome you to come together with ASU alumni, fans, and members of the military community as we remember and honor the legacy of Pat Tillman. This year, all adult registrants will receive an official 2025 Pat's Run race shirt and race medal; kid's run participants will receive the kid's run shirt and kid's finishing award. All race packets will be shipped and tracking information will be shared upon fulfillment, which will come closer to the time leading up to race day. If you have any questions about this race, please contact the race director at TillmanHonorRuns@asu.edu

[Read More](/events-1/tillman-honor-run)

](/events-1/the-great-benjamins-circus-sunday-afternoon)

## [The Great Benjamins Circus - Sunday Afternoon](/events-1/the-great-benjamins-circus-sunday-afternoon)

*    3/16/2025 04:30 PM - 3/16/2025 06:00 PM
*     1045 LOWER GOLD CAMP RD

A traditional circus that offers amazing acts of international quality while being true to the traditional circus roots. Great Benjamins Circus and its caravans travel to the four corners of North America; from Florida to California, Alaska to Newfoundland / Labrador. Benjamins' offers a rich and sincere circus experience. Much more than an entertainment organization, we present ourselves as being a symbol of respect and education toward all cultures. We are an ambassador of multiculturalism and have a great appreciation for performance art. Join our family for 90 minutes of entertainment and experience 90 minutes of energy, beauty, and excitement. Children under 2 are free as long as they sit on your lap.

[Read More](/events-1/the-great-benjamins-circus-sunday-afternoon)

*    3/16/2025 10:00 AM - 3/16/2025 05:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

This comprehensive event brings together homeowners and many of the most knowledgeable and experienced remodeling, landscaping and building experts. Every aspect of the home can be explored, from the smallest design detail to the largest house addition. Professionals will be on-hand to share their valuable knowledge to bring your ideas to life or inspire you with new ones. See the latest innovations and design trends! Experts will be showcasing everything for the home including, but not limited to, the latest in cabinetry and countertops, flooring, sunrooms and additions, basement finishing, waterproofing, smart home automation, energy efficient windows and exterior products, as well as the newest ideas pertaining to landscape installations. Be inspired by the feature gardens built right inside the exhibit hall - a great precursor to spring! Make plans to visit with the most knowledgeable experts in the area at the Colorado Springs Remodeling Expo. This all-encompassing event will surely put you on the path towards making your dream home a reality. As an added bonus, parking is free. We look forward to seeing you at the Norris-Penrose Event Center this March! Visit on Friday from 2-7, Saturday 10-7, and Sunday 10-5. Tickets are only $5 and kids 18 and under are FREE! Benjamins' offers a rich and sincere circus experience. Much more than an entertainment organization, we present ourselves as being a symbol of respect and education toward all cultures. We are an ambassador of multiculturalism and have a great appreciation for performance art. Join our family for 90 minutes of entertainment and experience 90 minutes of energy, beauty, and excitement. Children under 2 are free as long as they sit on your lap.

[Read More](/events-1/the-great-benjamins-circus-saturday-evening)

## [The Broadmoor Winter Polo Classic](/events-1/the-broadmoor-winter-polo-classic)

*    2/22/2025 02:30 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

The Broadmoor Winter Polo Classic brings winter polo to residents across the Pikes Peak region on February 22, 2025 at the Norris Penrose Event Center – brought to you by Los Hermanos Ranch and Colorado Springs Sports Corporation. The match will once again feature top polo players from around the country. The Broadmoor Winter Polo Classic will adhere to most of the standard, traditional polo regulations with the exception of the tournament taking place in a dirt arena instead of a grass field. Teams will be comprised of three players and play will consist of four 7-min chukkers (periods).

Select your seats, choose your experience, and rest assured with our secure checkout. Don't miss out – saddle up your spot today and be part of a new reign on rodeo! Your Adventure Starts Here Dust off your boots, tip your hat, and join us for a weekend where every moment counts. The Pioneer isn't just a rodeo; it's where legends come alive, and you're invited to be part of the story.

Feel the thrill of the ride and the warmth of giving back. Easy, Secure, and Quick Ticketing starts June 1st! Getting your hands on The Pioneer tickets is as smooth as a cowboy's draw. Select your seats, choose your experience, and rest assured with our secure checkout. Don't miss out – saddle up your spot today and be part of a new reign on rodeo! Your Adventure Starts Here Dust off your boots, tip your hat, and join us for a weekend where every moment counts. The Pioneer isn't just a rodeo; it's where legends come alive, and you're invited to be part of the story.

[Read More](/events-1/the-pioneer-a-new-reign-of-rodeo-friday-night)

You will find displays of fossils, meteorites, sculptures, jewelry, crystals, and more - including the 2024 feature Petrified Wood! Admission is $5 per day, $8 for multiple days, and kids 12 and under are Free.

[Read More](/events-1/60th-annual-pikes-peak-gem-mineral-jewelry-show)

13Apr

](/events-1/pat-tillman-honor-run-1)

## [Pat Tillman Honor Run](/events-1/pat-tillman-honor-run-1)

*    4/13/2024 09:00 AM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Join the ASU Colorado Chapter for the Tillman Honor Run on Saturday, April 13th at 9 AM. The ASU Alumni Association, in partnership with the Pat Tillman Foundation, coordinates the annual Tillman Honor Runs around the U.S. each April to honor the legacy and impact of former Sun Devil and Army Ranger Pat Tillman. These 4.2-mile fun runs and walks are hosted by ASU Alumni chapters and clubs in multiple cities across the U.S. The 4.2-mile race route planned in each city represents the No. 42 Pat wore on his jersey as a Sun Devil scholar-athlete. This family-friendly run/walk is open to all ages and all participants receive a medal and a Pat’s Run race shirt. By registering for a local run, funds are raised for the Pat Tillman Foundation's Tillman Scholars program, which helps our nation’s active-duty service members, veterans, and military spouses achieve their dream education. Visit https://alumni.asu.edu/events/tillman-honor-runs to register today!

[Read More](/events-1/pat-tillman-honor-run-1)

As a result of resoundingly positive feedback from the Colorado Springs community, The Broadmoor Winter Polo Classic will be back for a second year to bring winter polo to residents across the Pikes Peak region. The Broadmoor and Colorado Springs Sports Corporation are pleased to bring the fanfare and glamour of polo back to the Colorado Springs community. The 2024 endeavor will take place at the Norris Penrose Event Center on February 24, 2024. The match will once again feature top polo players from around the country. The Broadmoor Winter Polo Classic will adhere to most of the standard, traditional polo regulations with the exception of the tournament taking place in a dirt arena instead of a grass field. Teams will be comprised of three players and play will consist of four 7-min chukkers (periods).

[Read More](/events-1/winter-horse-polo-classic)

This free event will be showcasing over 150 exhibitors and great live music. Browse through a treasure trove of handcrafted wonders, perfect for finding unique gifts or indulging in a little something special for yourself. Engage with talented local artists and makers, who will be eager to share their stories and inspiration behind their creations. Mark your calendars and join us for a seasonal celebration of creativity that will add a touch of magic to your holiday season. Visit ColoradoEvents.org for vendor information and applications. Hours: Sunday from 10 am - 5 PM.

## [Pawtoberfest](/events-1/pawtoberfest)

*    9/16/2023 09:00 AM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Dogs and beer! Pawtoberfest is one of Colorado's ONLY dog-friendly festivals, featuring craft brew and spirit tastings with over 30 beverage partners, a 2-mile dog walk, pet-friendly vendors, music, pet contests, activities for the whole family, and plenty of scrumptious food from local food trucks! The walk starts near the South parking lot and ends at the festival. Dog-friendly shuttle buses will start at 9:30 a.m. and run throughout the day from the Norris Penrose parking lot to the Festival at Bear Creek Regional Park (West) for those wishing to skip the walk and proceed directly to the Festival.

13Jul

](/events-1/nfr-open-at-the-pikes-peak-or-bust-rodeo-2)

## [NFR Open at the Pikes Peak or Bust Rodeo](/events-1/nfr-open-at-the-pikes-peak-or-bust-rodeo-2)

*    7/13/2023 04:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

ProRodeo World Champions & rising stars from across the U.S. and Canadian Circuits will compete in the shadow of America’s Mountain for over $1 million in payouts. It’s all happening at the NFR Open at the Pikes Peak or Bust Rodeo, July 11-15, 2023 in Colorado Springs, Colorado! More than 200 contestants from across the 12 U.S. Circuits (plus Canada) will be bringing their talents to scenic and historic Norris Penrose Event Center, competing in Bareback Riding, Breakaway Roping, Steer Wrestling, Team Roping, Saddle Bronc Riding, Tie-down Roping, Barrel Racing, and Bull Riding, culminating with semifinals and finals on Saturday, July 15 when the national circuit champions will be crowned. Grounds open at 10:00 a.m. for the matinee performances and 4:00 p.m. for the evening performances. Arriving early helps with the crowd arriving at peak times and there is plenty to do before the Rodeo with a wide range of vendors offering food, drinks, apparel, and other Western fare, as well as the PPOBR Fan Zone with lots of family-friendly fun, plus some activities for the grown-ups too! After the Rodeo, live music and dancing await in the Coors Roadhouse Saloon so plan to stick around.

[Read More](/events-1/nfr-open-at-the-pikes-peak-or-bust-rodeo-2)

[

08Apr

](/events-1/pat-tillman-honor-run)

## [Pat Tillman Honor Run](/events-1/pat-tillman-honor-run)

*    4/8/2023 07:00 AM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Join fellow alumni for the Tillman Honor Run! Saturday, April 8th from 8am-10am. The ASU Alumni Association, in partnership with the Pat Tillman Foundation, coordinates the annual Tillman Honor Runs around the U.S. each April to honor the legacy and impact of former Sun Devil and Army Ranger Pat Tillman. These 4.2-mile fun runs and walks are hosted by ASU Alumni chapters and clubs in multiple cities across the U.S. The 4.2-mile race route planned in each city represents the No. 42 Pat wore on his jersey as a Sun Devil scholar-athlete. Family runs are open to all ages and all participants receive a medal and a Pat’s Run race shirt. By registering for a local run, funds are raised for the Pat Tillman Foundation's Tillman Scholars program, which helps our nation’s active-duty service members, veterans and military spouses achieve their dream education. Visit https://alumni.asu.edu/events/tillman-honor-runs to register for free today!

[Read More](/events-1/pat-tillman-honor-run)

## [L&L Colorado Springs Home & Landscape Expo](/events-1/l-l-colorado-springs-home-landscape-expo-3)

*    2/5/2023 10:00 AM - 2/5/2023 05:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Rd, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Welcome to the Home and Landscape Expo. This comprehensive event brings together homeowners and many of the most knowledgeable and experienced remodeling, landscaping and building experts. Every aspect of the home can be explored, from the smallest design detail to the largest house addition. Professionals will be on hand to share their valuable knowledge to bring your ideas to life or inspire you with new ones. See the latest innovations and design trends! Experts will be showcasing everything for the home including, but not limited to, the latest in cabinetry and countertops, flooring, sunrooms and additions, basement finishing, waterproofing, smart home automation, energy efficient windows and exterior products, as well as the newest ideas pertaining to landscape installations. Be inspired by the feature gardens built right inside the exhibit hall - a great precursor to spring! Make plans to visit with the most knowledgeable experts in the area at the Home and Landscape Expo. This all-encompassing event will surely put you on the path towards making your dream home a reality. As an added bonus, parking is free. We look forward to seeing you at the Norris-Penrose Event Center this February! FEATURED EXHIBITOR Jake’s Designs is an award winning, Colorado Springs based, landscape company established in 2000. Jake’s Designs specializes in guiding homeowners through the process of the landscape design all the way through the installation of the project. F&B Landscape, Inc.'s mission is to perform residential and commercial landscape installation and commercial landscaping maintenance services at the highest level. They believe in demonstrating integrity, quality, and efficiency while building and maintaining relationships with their neighbors in Colorado Springs and the surrounding areas. Sprinklers Inc & Landscaping offers the highest quality landscaping and sprinkler services at reasonable prices. Their goal is to surpass each and every one of our customer's expectations while staying within a competitive price range. Genesis Group's team of trained landscapers in Colorado Springs fulfills every single need of your project with superior craftsmanship and custom-built design. They create beautiful, custom landscapes, decks, cover, hardscapes and more! Innorev Home Solutions, LLC provides innovative composite decking and railling systems to meet your homes needs with low maintenance and high durability. Their composite decking is made with a unique combination of reclaimed wood and plastic, giving you the best qualities of both materials. DreamMaker Bath & Kitchen strives to provide our clients with the expertise and professionalism that is uncommon in the home remodeling industry. Bordner Home Improvement provides custom solutions to transform your home; from the highest-quality replacement windows, to luxurious bath remodels.No matter how large or small your home improvement needs, they are there to exceed your expectations from start to finish.

## [Tanner Gun Show](/events-1/tanner-gun-show-7)

*    1/29/2023 12:00 AM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

$15 FOR THREE DAYS OF THE COLORADO SPRINGS GUN SHOW: FRI 3-7 + SAT 9-5 + SUN 9-4 No refunds on any ticket purchases, for any reason, including weather, and sorry, no pets allowed at the show. Norris Penrose Event Center 1045 Lower Gold Camp Rd. Colorado Springs, CO 80905 We’ve been around since 1964 and we are now expanding into Colorado Springs with 500 tables at the newly remodeled, including a beautiful concrete floor, Norris Penrose Event Center. At our Colorado Springs gun show, you’ll find 500 tables with all types of guns, knives, ammunition, survival gear/food, and related hunting and fishing accessories including, holsters, western wear, gun safes, books, and war relics, and other collectibles. You are guaranteed the largest selection of old and new firearms in the state because we attract vendors from throughout the Western half of the U.S. Concealed carry permit classes are also available at each show through Effective Firearms Training. Classes are held once on Saturday and once on Sunday during the show. Please visit our concealed carry page for more information on the classes including pricing and times. DON’T FORGET THAT EVERY COLORADO SPRINGS GUN SHOW IS BUY, SELL, & TRADE! CUSTOMERS ARE ENCOURAGED TO BRING IN THEIR GUNS TO SELL, TRADE, OR GET AN APPRAISAL From the coolest gadgets and technologies to antiques and collector’s items, you’ll find it all here. Although the focus of the show is guns, knives, and outdoor equipment, there are also some other items for those not interested in guns, such as beef jerky, western wear, jewelry, artwork, and books. Additionally, there is a restaurant on-site with a large seating area. PLENTY OF FREE PARKING AVAILABLE! Strollers must be checked.

[Read More](/events-1/tanner-gun-show-7)

Their laminated timber walls and patented interlocking system are the perfect combination for a state-of-the-art home that lasts a lifetime. If you can dream it, they can create it. American Overhead Door's goal is to give you a garage door you can be proud of. They offer a wide variety of designs and functions that you can choose from, so you’ll know you’re getting a door to fit your needs perfectly. Your garage door is one of the first things you’ll see that’ll remind you that you’re home. When you have a beautiful garage door awaiting your arrival, it makes the transition from a long day at work to an evening of relaxation that much sweeter. Affinity Mortgage Brokers is a premier mortgage broker located in Colorado Springs. We are veteran owned with 50+ years combined experience in the mortgage industry. Purely Ponds Fine Landscapes designs and installs complete landscapes in addition to water features. The Purely Ponds team thrives because of customer referrals, which means anything less than total customer satisfactions is unacceptable. They offer xeriscaping, outdoor kitchens, patios and fine landscape options. Granite Transformations is a premier home and businesses renovation company specializing in kitchens and bathrooms. They have performed many renovations using a unique process which requires little-to-no demolition. This process reduces the stress of a typical intrusive and disruptive installation, along with the problems and setbacks typical of such home improvement projects. They have a wide variety of top-notch products that are designed to fit any project and any budget. Vision Sync Home Improvement is a premiere home remodeling company accepting jobs throughout the Front Range as far north as Cheyenne, WY and as far south as Pueblo, CO. Vision Sync Home Improvement LLC has chosen to work with only the most reliable and top of the line suppliers that can get material needed to customers specifications in as little as four business days. HOMEfix is a home remodeling, handyman repair, and roofing company serving the Greater Colorado Springs area, including Monument, Fountain, Woodland Park, and Falcon. Services include kitchen remodeling, bathroom remodeling, basement finishing, roof replacement & repair, solar installation, HVAC installation & repair, painting services, drywall repair, flooring installation, siding repair, tile repair, deck repair, custom carpentry, fence replacement, and more. The team of HVAC contractors from Mitchell Heating Colorado has, with dedication, serving residential and commercial property owners in Colorado Springs, Denver, and Northern Colorado with their heating and cooling service needs for many years. You can trust us for all your heating and cooling service needs, whether you need repair, maintenance, or replacement. We will ensure that your Colorado Springs property does not go without a working HVAC system when you need it most!

01Jan

](/events-1/pikes-peak-road-runners-rescue-run)

## [Pikes Peak Road Runners Rescue Run](/events-1/pikes-peak-road-runners-rescue-run)

*    1/1/2023 10:00 AM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Welcome back to Norris-Penrose Events Center / Bear Creek Park! In the beginning, Jan 1, 1979, the Rescue Run Start/Finish was at Norris-Penrose Events Center, then moved to Palmer Park in 1995. After many wonderful years at Palmer Park, we are returning back to our roots! But rather than run on the streets, as we did then, we will be running on the trails of Bear Creek Park East. We are excited to be back with a new course to welcome in the New Year for our 45th time! These items of interest were new last year and are returning for this year's race: NEW LAST YEAR: Start/Finish location change to: Norris-Penrose Events Center 1045 Lower Gold Camp Rd, Colorado Springs, CO 80905 NEW LAST YEAR: 5K and 10K start together at 10:00am. If you prefer a more spread out start, the start will be available for approximately 10 minutes after the group start. Please cross starting line timing mats by 10:10am No race day registration. Online registration ends Dec 31, 6:00PM Mountain Time. Please expect ice in places on trails, and especially wooden bridges. Wearing shoe traction is at your discretion 1045 Lower Gold Camp Rd, Colorado Springs, CO 80905 Map to the start and parking RAIN, SNOW, OR 20° BELOW Welcome runners and walkers to our 45th year! EL PASO COUNTY SEARCH AND RESCUE (EPCSAR) Proceeds of this event go to El Paso County Search and Rescue (EPCSAR). EPCSAR provides search and rescue services primarily in the jurisdiction of El Paso County, Colorado. However, the team also responds to requests for assistance from other counties through a cooperative arrangement with the Colorado Search and Rescue Board. From the summit of Pikes Peak to the plains or eastern El Paso County and anywhere in between, the EPCSAR team is ready to help. Responding to calls for those who are lost, injured, or ill on a trail or in the forest, stranded, lost, or experiencing a medical emergency during a blizzard, any time of day or night, these dedicated volunteers stand ready to respond, 24/7/365. All members purchase their own equipment and contribute hundreds of volunteer hours to the community. EPCSAR never charges for their services. Donations made during race registration will NOT be charged a processing fee. RACE FEES / SCHEDULE / IMPORTANT INFORMATION Register online until Dec 31. No race day registration. 5K and 10K start together at 10:00 am January 1. Register early for savings and to guarantee shirt availability! Now - Nov. 30 - $25 Entry Fee / $20 optional shirt. Dec 1 - Dec 31 - $35 Entry Fee / $20 optional Shirt NOTE - You must be pre-registered by Dec 31 at 6:00 PM Mountain Time. There is NO RACE DAY REGISTRATION Please email rescuerun@epcsar.org , or call/text 719-351-0045, if you are unable to preregister and we will assist you personally. Long sleeve tech shirt add-on is optional during signup. Sizes are unisex, XS - XXL. Please sign up early, in November, to guarantee shirt availability. Our supplier has warned us of possible shortages. Shirts and bibs will be available for pickup in December. You will be notified when your bib has been delivered if you are not picking it up on race day. Free: Kids runs starting at 9:30, three age-appropriate distances Switching Distance: Switching is allowed at no charge prior to the race. It is preferable that you do so online within runsignup.com before Dec 31. If you decide to switch at the race start, please let a timing official know prior to the race. Not doing so slows down awards and results. And it might be cold out there! Dogs: Pets are not allowed on the course per PPRR/RRCA rules. This is especially important on our 10K course as a portion of this course is on private property with the agreement from the landowner that we will not allow dogs. In order to secure the future use of the property, if you attempt to run the 10K with a dog, you will be stopped during the race. Trail Etiquette: No litter / No cutting corners / No off-trail travel. Any activities of this nature may result in disqualification. Please understand our stance on this in order to continue conducting this race on a mixture of private and public lands. Change in COVID protocol: We will follow and may implement, changes in our COVID protocols, up to and including canceling in-person events. If necessary, the race could be changed to a virtual event. Please understand, there will be no refunds. RACE CONTACT INFO If you have any questions about this race, please contact the race director at rescuerun@epcsar.org

[Read More](/events-1/pikes-peak-road-runners-rescue-run)

The 24th Annual Holiday Food & Gift Festival returns to Colorado Springs December 2-4 at the Norris Penrose Event Center. This Festival is one of the largest holiday celebrations in the West, with shoppers who happily complete their holiday shopping early. Carmel Veterans Service Center offers a variety of wellness programs that address behavioral/mental, spiritual and physical health at little to no cost. More Information about the Expo: https://www.womenslivingexpo.com/colorado-springs-2022/ Show hours: Saturday November 5, 20212 - 10am to 5pm Sunday November 6, 2022 - 11am to 4pm. SAVE $2 with Expo Tickets purchased here! Tickets: $7 general admission in person (or $5 purchased here), children 8 and under free. Parking is Free. Tickets are also available at our Box Office during Expo Hours. More Expo information is available at our event Website: https://www.womenslivingexpo.com/colorado-springs-2022/

## [Tanner Gun Show](/events-1/tanner-gun-show-4)

*    10/23/2022 09:00 AM - 10/23/2022 04:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

$15 FOR THREE DAYS OF THE COLORADO SPRINGS GUN SHOW: FRI 3-7 + SAT 9-5 + SUN 9-4 We’ve been around since 1964 and we have expanded into Colorado Springs with 500 tables at the newly remodeled, including a beautiful concrete floor, Norris Penrose Event Center. At our Colorado Springs gun show, you’ll find 500 tables with all types of guns, knives, ammunition, survival gear/food, and related hunting and fishing accessories including, holsters, western wear, gun safes, books, and war relics, and other collectibles. You are guaranteed the largest selection of old and new firearms in the state because we attract vendors from throughout the Western half of the U.S. Concealed carry permit classes are also available at each show through Effective Firearms Training. Classes are held once on Saturday and once on Sunday during the show. Please visit our concealed carry page for more information on the classes including pricing and times. DON’T FORGET THAT EVERY COLORADO SPRINGS GUN SHOW IS BUY, SELL, & TRADE! CUSTOMERS ARE ENCOURAGED TO BRING IN THEIR GUNS TO SELL, TRADE, OR GET AN APPRAISAL From the coolest gadgets and technologies to antiques and collector’s items, you’ll find it all here. Although the focus of the show is guns, knives, and outdoor equipment, there are also some other items for those not interested in guns, such as beef jerky, western wear, jewelry, artwork, and books. Additionally, there is a restaurant on-site with a large seating area. PLENTY OF FREE PARKING IS AVAILABLE!

We proudly accomplished a successful 6 years with record crowds and plan on making 2022 EVEN BIGGER!! We couldn't be more pleased with the lineup of breweries, wine, and distilleries that have joined the movement to put on an amazing experience this October. The Haunted Brew Fest will feature beverages from some of Colorado's best breweries, wine distributors, and distilleries around the region. In order to enter The Haunted Brew Fest, you must prove that you are 21 years of age or older by showing legal identification at the door regardless if you appear to be over the age of 21. No ID, No Entry, No Exceptions. (This includes relatives, friends, and employees). No Children will be allowed. Have your ID ready to be checked at the door.

## [Pikes Peak Road Runners Fall Series Race](/events-1/pikes-peak-road-runners-fall-series-race)

*    10/2/2022 10:00 AM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

This event has been around since 1979. Larry Miller has been the race director for a couple of decades. His enthusiasm for laying out challenging cross country courses has never wavered, and he always has something new up his sleeve. The running community is certain that Larry lays awake at night thinking of new twists for the Fall Series courses. There have been hay bale jumps, fence climbing, culvert tunnels and more. And because of all of this, we all have stories to pass along. Thank you, Larry! In 2017, Larry Miller, Pat Lockhart, Kirk Brown and Stewart Cooper are teaming up as co-race directors. They will bring the best of the best for the Fall Series.

## [Pawtober Fest](/events-1/pawtober-fest)

*    9/17/2022 09:00 AM - 9/17/2022 11:00 AM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

Dogs and beer! Pawtoberfest is one of Colorado's ONLY dog-friendly festivals, featuring craft brew and spirit tastings with over 30 beverage partners, a 2-mile dog walk, pet-friendly vendors, music, pet contests, activities for the whole family, and plenty of scrumptious food from local food trucks! Pawtoberfest brings animal lovers and dogs together for an important purpose - to help Humane Society of the Pikes Peak Region care for thousands of lost, abandoned, abused, and unwanted pets, giving them a second chance. Our Dog Walk will take place at Bear Creek Regional Park - EAST and will begin at 9 a.m. All Parking is at Norris Penrose Event Center. The Walk starts near the parking lot and finishes at the Festival. Participants may walk the 2-mile Dog Walk at their leisure between the hours of 9:00 a.m. - 11:00 a.m. The Festival will take place at Bear Creek Regional Park-WEST from 10 a.m. -3:00 p.m. with FREE Dog-Friendly Shuttle Bus service from the parking lot at Norris Penrose to the festival starting at 9:30 a.m. and concluding at 3:15 p.m. More info & tickets at www.pawtoberfest.org ​Vendor booths are still available. Sign up at : https://secure.qgiv.com/for/2pvsr

[Read More](/events-1/pawtober-fest)

## [Ride for the Brand Ranch Rodeo](/events-1/ride-for-the-brand-ranch-rodeo-1)

*    8/6/2022 04:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Road, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

If you are looking for a real western experience, don’t miss the Ride for the Brand Ranch Rodeo on Saturday, August 6 at Norris Penrose Event Center. Ride for the Brand is one of the largest ranch rodeo sanctioned by the Working Ranch Cowboys Association and features teams of real working cowboys who work all week on their ranches, sorting and moving cattle, starting colts, doctoring and branding calves. You will see teams compete in events like Ranch Bronc Riding, Wild Cow Milking, Stray Gathering and the fan favorite – Trailer Loading. And when the rodeo is over, we’re going to dance in the dirt with live music from Jason Wulf. Gates open at 4 p.m., the Ride for the Brand Ranch Rodeo starts at 6 p.m. and the Dance in the Dirt starts when the rodeo wraps-up at 8 p.m. Learn more at rideforthebrand.org.

## [81st Pikes Peak or Bust Rodeo | NFR Open](/events-1/81st-pikes-peak-or-bust-rodeo-nfr-open-2)

*    7/16/2022 04:00 PM
*     1045 Lower Gold Camp Rd, Colorado Springs, CO, USA

The best in PRORODEO are coming to Colorado Springs as the 81st Cinch Pikes Peak or Bust Rodeo welcomes the 2022 NFR Open, July 13-16, 2022 at scenic Norris Penrose Event Center! The inaugural NFR Open powered by RAM will be one of the biggest rodeos of the 2022 PRORODEO season with $1 million in payouts. More than 200 contestants from each of the 12 U.S. Circuits (plus Canada) will be competing in Bareback Riding, Steer Wrestling, Team Roping, Saddle Bronc Riding, Tie-down Roping, Breakway Roping, Barrel Racing and Bull Riding, culminating with a semifinals and finals on Saturday, July 16 when the national circuit champions will be crowned. All evening performances begin at 6:45 p.m., grounds open at 4:00 p.m., and parking is FREE!! Arrive early for family-friendly fun and attractions in the PPOBR Fan Zone along with a wide range of vendors offering food, drinks, apparel and other Western fare—and stay after the Rodeo for live music and dancing in the Coors Roadhouse Saloon! \*PLEASE NOTE, THESE TICKETS ARE FOR THE EVENING PERFORMANCE ON SATURDAY JULY 16, 2022 ONLY\* 2022 PERFORMANCE/THEME SPONSORS Saturday Evening, July 16, 2022—NORAD/USNORTHCOM Night Sponsored by Lockheed Martin TICKETING-\*\*ALL TICKETS ARE NON-REFUNDABLE, EVENT IS RAIN OR SHINE\*\*

[Read More](/events-1/81st-pikes-peak-or-bust-rodeo-nfr-open-2)

## RV/Camper & Other Services

* * *

*   [All](#)

#### **OVERNIGHTS**

RV, Camper, and Horse Stalls are available on-site. For additional information and pricing details, please click 'Learn More'. Contact the Campground Host at (719) 425-0468 or email at campground@norrispenrose.com

[Learn More](/rv-camper-other-services/overnights)

#### **SUMMIT CATERING**

Our onsite caterers are excited to help meet all of your event needs! Summit Catering Specializes in Custom Menu and Event Design. For more information on Summit, please contact them: taste@summitcatering.net 719-229-6466 http://www.summitcatering.net/

[Learn More](/rv-camper-other-services/summit-catering)

#### **7 Falls Shuttle Service**

All Visitors to Seven Falls must park at 1045 Lower Gold Camp Rd, Colorado Springs (The Norris Penrose Event Center). From there, visitors to the Broadmoor Seven Falls enjoy complimentary shuttle service to the falls from the parking area on the north side of the Norris Penrose stadium arena. For tickets and information please visit www.sevenfalls.com or call 855-923-7272.

[Learn More](https://www.sevenfalls.com/)

## Facility Descriptions

* * *

*   [All](#)

#### **STADIUM ARENA**

The stadium arena hosts an array of events from May to October; including rodeos, equestrian events, motorsports, circuses, cross country meets, concerts, and festivals.

[Learn More](/facility-descriptions/stadium-arena)

#### **INDOOR EVENT CENTER**

This indoor space has been redesigned to increase its capacity to host a variety of non-equestrian events, including home and landscaping shows, gun shows, brew fests, dog shows, motorcycle shows, business expos, private parties and weddings. Renovations completed in 2020!

[Learn More](/facility-descriptions/indoor-event-center)

#### **Heritage Room**

This multipurpose event space offers a one of a kind setting, commonly used for banquets, meetings, and receptions. It is offered in conjunction with stadium events or can be used on its own for private functions.

[Learn More](/facility-descriptions/heritage-room)

## Directions

* * *

![Directions image](https://images.cdn-files-a.com/uploads/1423563/400_5b86f246edbd9.png)

**From the North:**  
Travel I-25 south to Exit 141 for US-24 W toward Cimarron Street. Keep right at the fork to continue on W Cimarron St. Use the left 2 lanes to turn left toward S 8th Street. Turn left onto 8th St. Turn right onto Lower Gold Camp Rd (Norris Penrose Event Center will be on the left)  
  
**From the South:**  
Travel I-25 north to Exit 141 for US-24 W toward Cimarron Street. Turn left onto W Cimarron Street and use the left 2 lanes to turn left toward S 8th Street. Turn left onto S 8th St. Turn. Turn right onto Lower Gold Camp Rd (Norris Penrose Event Center will be on the left).

## About

* * *

The Norris Penrose Event Center has been at this location since 1973 and under current ownership since 2003. Proud home of the Pikes Peak or Bust Rodeo and part of the Spencer Penrose legacy of Colorado Springs, the Norris Penrose Event Center is focused on preserving Western Heritage, raising funds for our local enlisted military personnel, and providing top class event facilities of all kinds. The newest renovation to our property is the Indoor Event Center which provides up to 50,000 square feet of event space and was remodeled to accommodate all varieties of trade shows, festivals and fundraisers.  
  
The Norris Penrose Event Center is owned by a 501(c)(3) non-profit and works with our sister events to help raise funds for our local military. We recently partnered with Seven Falls to provide offsite shuttle service and also work with Cheyenne Mountain Zoo and other local nonprofits to help all of our local events and activities flourish.  
  
As Colorado Springs' Premier Event Center we are ready to help put on shows and events of all kinds!

![About image](https://images.cdn-files-a.com/uploads/1423563/400_5f03a60b9e741.jpg)
